 456661 1833615       01:15 Ss   lelinfe+ /bin/bash -lc ev='本地实验/R-W4-4/V100/partial-ranking' build="$ev/stage/build-v100" source /usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/set_env.sh source_rc=$? export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:+:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH}" result=PASS printf 'REVISION=V100\nGATE=INTERLEAVED_LOCAL\nPARENT=R31B-V011\nPARENT_SHA256=a8c19a1972207acc67e3fb0cd393cc70b0a4b183d1eaf5610edf80c2879b15e3\nCANDIDATE_CUTOFF=992\nDEVICE=3\nDTYPE=FP32\nSHAPES=128x988,128x992,128x996\nWARMUP=45\nSAMPLES_PER_BLOCK=31\nBLOCKS=2\nGAP_SEC=0\nBATCH_N=64\nPAIRS_PER_SHAPE=6\nORDER_PER_SHAPE=01:P,C;02:C,P;03:P,C;04:C,P;05:P,C;06:C,P\nPARTIAL_CORRECTNESS=YES\nLOCAL_SCORE_COMPARABLE_TO_OFFICIAL=NO\nC15=EXCLUDED_KNOWN_PARENT_FAILURE\nENV_SOURCE_RC=%s\nSTART_UTC=%s\n' "$source_rc" "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" > "$ev/v100-local-command-results.log" snapshot_pair() {   pair="$1"   width="$2"   phase="$3"   stem="$ev/local-pair${pair}-r128-w${width}-${phase}"   date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ > "${stem}-timestamp.txt"   npu-smi info > "${stem}-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-npu-smi-info.rc"   npu-smi info -t usages -i 3 > "${stem}-usage.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-usage.rc"   ps -eo pid=,ppid=,etime=,stat=,user=,args= > "${stem}-processes.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-ps.rc" } run_one() {   pair="$1"   width="$2"   variant="$3"   binary="$build/clx_ref_${variant}_probe"   prefix="$ev/local-pair${pair}-${variant}-r128-w${width}-fp32"   start=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   printf 'START_UTC=%s\nDEVICE=3\nSHAPE=FP32_128x%s\nCOMMAND=%s 3 128 %s 0 %s 45 31 2 0 64\n' "$start" "$width" "$binary" "$width" "$prefix" > "${prefix}-event.txt"   "$binary" 3 128 "$width" 0 "$prefix" 45 31 2 0 64 > "${prefix}-stdout.log" 2> "${prefix}-stderr.log"   rc=$?   line=$(rg -m1 '^REF_HARNESS ' "${prefix}-stdout.log" || true)   bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$line" | sed -n 's/.* bad=\([0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p')   max_abs=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="max_abs" {print $2; exit}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null)   [ -n "$bad" ] || bad=UNKNOWN   [ -n "$max_abs" ] || max_abs=UNKNOWN   end=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   printf 'END_UTC=%s\nRESULT=%s\nRC=%s\nBAD=%s\nMAX_ABS=%s\n' "$end" "$line" "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" >> "${prefix}-event.txt"   printf 'PAIR=%s WIDTH=%s VARIANT=%s RC=%s BAD=%s MAX_ABS=%s END_UTC=%s\n' "$pair" "$width" "$variant" "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" "$end" | tee -a "$ev/v100-local-command-results.log"   if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] || [ "$bad" != 0 ]; then result=FAIL; fi } for width in 988 992 996; do   for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do     pair=$(printf '%02d' "$i")     snapshot_pair "$pair" "$width" pre     if [ $((i % 2)) -eq 1 ]; then       run_one "$pair" "$width" parent       run_one "$pair" "$width" candidate     else       run_one "$pair" "$width" candidate       run_one "$pair" "$width" parent     fi     snapshot_pair "$pair" "$width" post     printf 'PAIR_DONE WIDTH=%s PAIR=%s STATUS=%s\n' "$width" "$pair" "$result" | tee -a "$ev/v100-local-command-results.log"   done done printf 'LOCAL_RUN_RESULT=%s\nEND_UTC=%s\n' "$result" "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" | tee -a "$ev/v100-local-command-results.log" test "$result" = PASS
